IAS 39             Financial Instruments: Recognition and Measurement                                                                   IFRS 5    IFRS 6      IFRS 7

Overview           Sets out the requirements for hedge accounting. An entity can elect to apply these requirements or those in          IFRS 8    IFRS 9     IFRS 10
                   IFRS 9.                                                                                                              IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13
Hedge accounting   Hedge accounting (recognising the offsetting effects of both the hedging instrument and the hedged item in           IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
                   the same period’s profit or loss) is permitted if the hedging relationship is clearly designated and documented,
                                                                                                                                        IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
                   measurable and effective.
                                                                                                                                         IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10
                   Because IFRS 9 includes only general hedge accounting requirements, the requirements on portfolio hedges in
                                                                                                                                        IAS 12    IAS 16      IAS 19
                   IAS 39 remain applicable.
                                                                                                                                        IAS 20    IAS 21      IAS 23
Fair value hedge   When there is a hedge of a change in fair value of a recognised asset or liability or firm commitment, the
                   change in fair values of both the hedging instrument and the hedged item for the designated risk are                 IAS 24    IAS 26      IAS 27
                   recognised in profit or loss when they occur and the carrying amount of the hedged item is adjusted to reflect       IAS 28    IAS 29      IAS 32
                   changes in the hedged risk.
                                                                                                                                        IAS 33    IAS 34      IAS 36
Cash flow hedge    When an entity hedges changes in the future cash flows relating to a recognised asset or liability or a highly
                                                                                                                                        IAS 37    IAS 38      IAS 39
                   probable forecast transaction that involves a party external to the entity, or a firm commitment in some
                   cases, then the change in fair value of the hedging instrument is recognised in other comprehensive income           IAS 40    IAS 41     IFRIC 12
                   to the extent that the hedge is effective until such time as the hedged future cash flows occur.                    IFRIC 17    PS 1           PS 2

                                                                                                                                             IFRS 1    IFRS 2      IFRS 3
Hedge of a net      This relates to a net investment in a foreign operation (as defined in IAS 21), including a hedge of a monetary
investment in a     item that is accounted for as part of the net investment. The accounting for such a hedge is similar to a cash           IFRS 5    IFRS 6      IFRS 7
foreign entity      flow hedge.                                                                                                              IFRS 8    IFRS 9     IFRS 10
Intragroup hedges The foreign currency risk of a highly probable forecast intragroup transaction can qualify as the hedged item              IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13
                  in a cash flow hedge in the consolidated financial statements, provided that the transaction is denominated
                                                                                                                                             IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
                  in a currency other than the functional currency of the entity entering into that transaction and the foreign
                  currency risk will affect the consolidated profit or loss.                                                                 IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
                                                                                                                                              IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10
                    If the hedge of a forecast intragroup transaction qualifies for hedge accounting, any gain or loss that is
                                                                                                                                             IAS 12    IAS 16      IAS 19
                    recognised in other comprehensive income in accordance with the hedging rules in IAS 39 is reclassified
                    from equity to profit or loss in the same period or periods in which the foreign currency risk of the hedged             IAS 20    IAS 21      IAS 23
                    transaction affects profit or loss.                                                                                      IAS 24    IAS 26      IAS 27
Portfolio hedge     A portfolio hedge of interest rate risk (hedging an amount rather than a specific asset or liability) can qualify as
                                                                                                                                             IAS 28    IAS 29      IAS 32
                    a fair value hedge or a cash flow hedge if specified conditions are met.
                                                                                                                                             IAS 33    IAS 34      IAS 36
Interpretations     None

IAS 40       Investment Property                                                                                                       IFRS 5    IFRS 6      IFRS 7

Overview     Prescribes the accounting when property is held to earn rentals or for capital appreciation rather than being             IFRS 8    IFRS 9     IFRS 10
             occupied by the owner for the production or supply of goods or services or for administrative purposes.                   IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13
Investment   An investment property is land or buildings (or part thereof) or both held (whether by the owner or by a lessee           IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
property     under a finance lease) to earn rentals or for capital appreciation or both.
                                                                                                                                       IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
             IAS 40 does not apply to owner-occupied property, property that is being constructed or developed on behalf of             IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10
             third parties, property held for sale in the ordinary course of business or property that is leased to another entity
                                                                                                                                       IAS 12    IAS 16      IAS 19
             under a finance lease.
                                                                                                                                       IAS 20    IAS 21      IAS 23
             Mixed-use property (partly used by the owner and partly held for rental or appreciation) must be split with
                                                                                                                                       IAS 24    IAS 26      IAS 27
             components accounted for separately if these portions could be sold separately.
                                                                                                                                       IAS 28    IAS 29      IAS 32
             A property interest held by a lessee under an operating lease can qualify as investment property if the lessee            IAS 33    IAS 34      IAS 36
             applies the fair value model. The lessee accounts for the lease as if it were a finance lease.
                                                                                                                                       IAS 37    IAS 38      IAS 39
             Property can be transferred in or out of investment property, but only if the entity has actually changed its             IAS 40    IAS 41     IFRIC 12
             use—intention to change is not sufficient. When an investment property carried at fair value is transferred to
                                                                                                                                      IFRIC 17    PS 1           PS 2
             owner-occupied property or inventories, the property’s fair value is the deemed cost for subsequent accounting in
             accordance with IAS 16 or IAS 2.

measurement   measurement model is applied to all of the entity’s investment property.                                           IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13

              Change from one model to the other is permitted if it will result in a more appropriate presentation (which is     IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
              highly unlikely for change from fair value to cost model).                                                         IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2

Fair value model   Investment property is measured at fair value and changes in fair value are recognised in profit or loss.
                                                                                                                                         IFRS 5    IFRS 6      IFRS 7
                   If an entity using the fair value model acquires a particular property for which there is clear evidence that the     IFRS 8    IFRS 9     IFRS 10
                   entity will not be able to determine fair value on a continuing basis, the cost model is used for that property—
                                                                                                                                         IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13
                   and it must continue to be used until disposal of the property.
Cost model         Investment property is measured at depreciated cost less any accumulated impairment losses unless it is               IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
                   classified as a non-current asset held for sale under IFRS 5. The fair value of the investment property must be       IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
                   disclosed.                                                                                                             IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10

IAS 41         Agriculture                                                                                                               IFRS 5    IFRS 6      IFRS 7
                                                                                                                                         IFRS 8    IFRS 9     IFRS 10
Overview       Prescribes the accounting for agricultural activity.
                                                                                                                                         IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13
Agricultural   Agricultural activity is the management of the biological transformation and harvest of biological assets for sale or
activity       for conversion into agricultural produce or into additional biological assets.                                            IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
                                                                                                                                         IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
               Bearer plants that are used in the production or supply of agricultural produce and which will not be sold as
               agricultural produce are accounted for as PP&E, applying IAS 16. These include fruit trees and grape vines.                IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10

Measurement    All biological assets are measured at fair value less costs to sell, unless fair value cannot be measured reliably.       IAS 12    IAS 16      IAS 19
                                                                                                                                         IAS 20    IAS 21      IAS 23
               Agricultural produce is measured at fair value less costs to sell at the point of harvest. Because harvested
               produce is a marketable commodity, there is no ‘measurement reliability’ exception for produce. Fair value                IAS 24    IAS 26      IAS 27
               measurement stops at harvest, after which IAS 2 applies.                                                                  IAS 28    IAS 29      IAS 32

               Any change in the fair value of biological assets during a period is reported in profit or loss.                          IAS 33    IAS 34      IAS 36
                                                                                                                                         IAS 37    IAS 38      IAS 39
                                                                                                                                         IAS 40    IAS 41     IFRIC 12
                                                                                                                                        IFRIC 17    PS 1           PS 2

IFRIC 12          Service Concession Arrangements
                                                                                                                                             IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
Overview          To address the accounting by private sector operators involved in the provision of public sector infrastructure             IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10
                  assets and services. The Interpretation does not address the accounting for the government (grantor) side of
                                                                                                                                             IAS 12    IAS 16      IAS 19
                  such arrangements.
Infrastructure    Infrastructure assets that are not controlled by an operator are not recognised as property, plant and equipment           IAS 20    IAS 21      IAS 23
assets            of the operator.                                                                                                           IAS 24    IAS 26      IAS 27

                  Instead, the operator recognises:                                                                                          IAS 28    IAS 29      IAS 32
                                                                                                                                             IAS 33    IAS 34      IAS 36
                  • A financial asset when the operator has an unconditional right to receive a specified amount of cash or
                    other financial asset over the life of the arrangement                                                                   IAS 37    IAS 38      IAS 39
                                                                                                                                             IAS 40    IAS 41     IFRIC 12
                  • An intangible asset when the operator’s future cash flows are not specified (e.g. when they will vary
                    according to usage of the infrastructure asset)                                                                         IFRIC 17    PS 1           PS 2

                  • Both a financial asset and an intangible asset when the operator’s return is provided partially by a financial asset
                    and partially by an intangible asset                                                                                                         173

IFRIC 17         Distributions of Non-cash Assets to Owners                                                                               IFRS 5    IFRS 6      IFRS 7
                                                                                                                                          IFRS 8    IFRS 9     IFRS 10
Overview         To address the accounting when non-cash assets are distributed to owners.
                                                                                                                                          IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13
Dividends        A dividend payable must be recognised when the dividend is appropriately authorised and is no longer at the
                 discretion of the entity.                                                                                                IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
                                                                                                                                          IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
                 An entity measures the non-cash dividend payable at the fair value of the assets to be distributed. The liability is
                 measured at each reporting date with changes recognised directly in equity.                                               IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10
                                                                                                                                          IAS 12    IAS 16      IAS 19
                 The difference between the dividend paid and the carrying amount of the assets distributed is recognised in profit
                 or loss.                                                                                                                 IAS 20    IAS 21      IAS 23

Practice         Management Commentary                                                                                                  IFRS 8    IFRS 9     IFRS 10
Statement 1                                                                                                                             IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13
Overview         Assists management in presenting useful management commentary that relates to financial statements that                IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
                 have been prepared in accordance with IFRS Standards.                                                                  IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
Status           The Practice Statement is not an IFRS Standard. Consequently, entities applying IFRS Standards are not required to      IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10
                 comply with the Practice Statement, unless specifically required by their jurisdiction.
                                                                                                                                        IAS 12    IAS 16      IAS 19
Management       Management commentary is defined in the Practice Statement as a narrative report that relates to financial
commentary       statements that have been prepared in accordance with IFRS Standards. Management commentary provides                   IAS 20    IAS 21      IAS 23
                 users with historical explanations of the amounts presented in the financial statements, specifically the              IAS 24    IAS 26      IAS 27
                 entity’s financial position, financial performance and cash flows. It also provides commentary on an entity’s          IAS 28    IAS 29      IAS 32
                 prospects and other information not presented in the financial statements. Management commentary also
                                                                                                                                        IAS 33    IAS 34      IAS 36
                 serves as a basis for understanding management’s objectives and its strategies for achieving those objectives.
                                                                                                                                        IAS 37    IAS 38      IAS 39
                                                                                                                                        IAS 40    IAS 41     IFRIC 12
                                                                                                                                       IFRIC 17    PS 1           PS 2

IFRS Accounting Standards, Interpretations and                                                                                  Introduction


Practice Statements                                                                                                             Deloitte
                                                                                                                              IFRS       Accounting
                                                                                                                                   Accounting
                                                                                                                              Interpretations
                                                                                                                                              Standards,
                                                                                                                                Research Tool and
                                                                                                                                              (DART)
                                                                                                                                                  Practice
                                                                                                                              Statements
                                                                                                                                Our IAS Plus website
                                                                                                                                IFRS 1    IFRS 2      IFRS 3
Presentation      Management commentary should be clear and straightforward and be presented with a focus on the most
                  important information in a manner intended to address the principles described in the Practice Statement,     IFRS 5    IFRS 6      IFRS 7
                  specifically:                                                                                                 IFRS 8    IFRS 9     IFRS 10
                                                                                                                                IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13
                  • Being consistent with its related financial statements
                                                                                                                                IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
                  • Avoiding duplicating disclosures made in the notes to the financial statements where practicable
                                                                                                                                IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
                  • Avoiding generic and immaterial disclosures                                                                  IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10
Elements of       Although the particular focus of management commentary will depend on the facts and circumstances of          IAS 12    IAS 16      IAS 19
management        the entity, management commentary should include information that is essential to an understanding of the
                                                                                                                                IAS 20    IAS 21      IAS 23
commentary        following five elements:
                                                                                                                                IAS 24    IAS 26      IAS 27
                  •   The nature of the business
                                                                                                                                IAS 28    IAS 29      IAS 32
                  •   Management’s objectives and its strategies for meeting those objectives
                  •   The entity’s most significant resources, risks and relationships                                          IAS 33    IAS 34      IAS 36
                  •   The results of operations and prospects                                                                   IAS 37    IAS 38      IAS 39
                  •   The critical performance measures and indicators that management uses to evaluate the entity’s
                                                                                                                                IAS 40    IAS 41     IFRIC 12
                      performance against stated objectives
                                                                                                                               IFRIC 17    PS 1           PS 2
Interpretations   None

Pending changes In May 2021, the IASB published ED/2021/6 Management Commentary, proposing a revision of the Practice               IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13
                Statement, as it has decided it should play a more active role in wider corporate reporting. In particular, as
                                                                                                                                    IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
                specialists in financial reporting, it concluded it is well placed to provide a link between financial and non-
                financial information. The IASB determined that an appropriate way to do this is a wholesale revision of the        IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
                Practice Statement.                                                                                                  IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10
                                                                                                                                    IAS 12    IAS 16      IAS 19
                    One of the key points that the IASB wishes to address is promoting alignment between financial and ‘other’
                    information disclosed by an entity. This new approach to management commentary could both encourage             IAS 20    IAS 21      IAS 23
                    and support further change, serving as an anchoring point for other updated standards and frameworks, for       IAS 24    IAS 26      IAS 27
                    example integrated reports or management reports required by jurisdictions.

Practice         Making Materiality Judgements                                                                                          IFRS 5    IFRS 6      IFRS 7
Statement 2
                                                                                                                                        IFRS 8    IFRS 9     IFRS 10
Overview         Assists management in presenting financial information about the entity that is useful to existing and                 IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13
                 potential investors, lenders and other creditors in making decisions about providing resources to the entity.
                                                                                                                                        IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
Status           The Practice Statement is not an IFRS Standard. Consequently, entities applying IFRS Standards are not required to
                                                                                                                                        IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
                 comply with the Practice Statement, unless specifically required by their jurisdiction.
                                                                                                                                         IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10
Definition of    Information is material if omitting or misstating it could influence decisions that users make on the basis of
material         financial information about a specific reporting entity. In other words, materiality is an entity-specific aspect      IAS 12    IAS 16      IAS 19
                 of relevance based on the nature or magnitude, or both, of the items to which the information relates in the           IAS 20    IAS 21      IAS 23
                 context of an individual entity’s financial report.                                                                    IAS 24    IAS 26      IAS 27
Pervasiveness    The Practice Statement notes that the need for materiality judgements is pervasive in the preparation of
                                                                                                                                        IAS 28    IAS 29      IAS 32
of materiality   financial statements and affects recognition, measurement, presentation and disclosure. Thus an entity
judgements       is only required to apply recognition and measurement requirements when the effect of applying them is                 IAS 33    IAS 34      IAS 36
                 material and need not provide a disclosure specified by an IFRS Standard if the information resulting from that        IAS 37    IAS 38      IAS 39
                 disclosure is not material.                                                                                            IAS 40    IAS 41     IFRIC 12
Judgement        When assessing whether information is material, an entity considers its own specific circumstances and the
                                                                                                                                       IFRIC 17    PS 1           PS 2
                 information needs of the primary users of its financial statements. Materiality judgements are reassessed at
                 each reporting date.

IFRS Accounting Standards, Interpretations and                                                                                         Introduction


Practice Statements                                                                                                                    Deloitte
                                                                                                                                     IFRS       Accounting
                                                                                                                                          Accounting
                                                                                                                                     Interpretations
                                                                                                                                                     Standards,

                                                                                                                                                         Practice
                                                                                                                                     Statements
                                                                                                                                       Our IAS Plus website
                                                                                                                                       IFRS 1    IFRS 2      IFRS 3
Primary users and   Primary users of an entity’s financial statements are existing and potential investors, lenders and other
their information   creditors. They can be expected to have a reasonable knowledge of business and economic activities and to          IFRS 5    IFRS 6      IFRS 7
needs               review and analyse the information included in the financial statements diligently. The objective of financial     IFRS 8    IFRS 9     IFRS 10
                    statements is to provide these primary users with financial information that is useful to them in making           IFRS 11   IFRS 12    IFRS 13
                    decisions about providing resources to the entity. Therefore, an entity also needs to consider what type of
                                                                                                                                       IFRS 14   IFRS 15    IFRS 16
                    decisions these users have to make. However, general purpose financial statements are not intended to
                    address specialised information needs, they focus on common information needs.                                     IFRS 17    IAS 1      IAS 2
Four-step process   The Practice Statement notes that an entity may find it helpful to follow a systematic process in making            IAS 7     IAS 8      IAS 10
                    materiality judgements and offers an example of such a process:                                                    IAS 12    IAS 16      IAS 19
                    • Step 1—The entity identifies information that has the potential to be material                                   IAS 20    IAS 21      IAS 23
                                                                                                                                       IAS 24    IAS 26      IAS 27
                    • Step 2—The entity assesses whether that information is material
                                                                                                                                       IAS 28    IAS 29      IAS 32
                    • Step 3—The entity organises the information within the draft financial statements in a manner that
                                                                                                                                       IAS 33    IAS 34      IAS 36
                      supports clear and concise communication
                                                                                                                                       IAS 37    IAS 38      IAS 39
                    • Step 4—The entity steps back and assesses the information provided in the draft financial statements as
                                                                                                                                       IAS 40    IAS 41     IFRIC 12
                      a whole
                                                                                                                                      IFRIC 17    PS 1           PS 2
Interpretations     None

IFRS S1             General Requirements for Disclosure of Sustainability-related Financial Information                                  IFRS Standards around
                                                                                                                                         the world
Overview            Sets out overall requirements with the objective of requiring an entity to disclose information about its
                    sustainability-related risks and opportunities that is useful to the primary users of general purpose financial      The IFRS Foundation
                    reports in making decisions relating to providing resources to the entity.                                           and its boards
Scope               An entity applies IFRS S1 in preparing and reporting sustainability-related financial disclosures in accordance
                                                                                                                                         IFRS Accounting Standards,
                    with IFRS Sustainability Disclosure Standards. An entity may apply IFRS Sustainability Disclosure Standards
                                                                                                                                         Interpretations and
                    irrespective of whether the entity’s related general purpose financial statements are prepared in accordance
                                                                                                                                         Practice Statements
                    with IFRS Accounting Standards or other generally accepted accounting principles or practices.
Conceptual          For sustainability-related financial information to be useful, it must be relevant and faithfully represent what   IFRS Sustainability Disclosure
foundations         it purports to represent. The usefulness of sustainability-related financial information is enhanced if the        Standards
                    information is comparable, verifiable, timely and understandable.
                                                                                                                                         IFRS Foundation projects
Fair presentation   A complete set of sustainability-related financial disclosures presents fairly all sustainability-related risks
                    and opportunities that could reasonably be expected to affect an entity’s prospects, and their faithful              Deloitte IFRS resources
                    representation. To achieve faithful representation, an entity is required to provide a complete, neutral and         Contacts
                    accurate depiction of those sustainability-related risks and opportunities.
Materiality         An entity is required to disclose material information about the sustainability-related risks and opportunities
                    that could reasonably be expected to affect the entity’s prospects. Information is material if omitting,
                    misstating or obscuring that information could reasonably be expected to influence decisions that primary
                    users of general purpose financial reports make on the basis of those reports.
                                                                                                                                                            183
IFRS Sustainability Disclosure Standards                                                                                                Introduction
                                                                                                                                        Deloitte Accounting
                                                                                                                                        Research Tool (DART)
Reporting entity   An entity’s sustainability-related financial disclosures are required to be for the same reporting entity as the
                   related financial statements.                                                                                        Our IAS Plus website

Connectivity       An entity is required to provide information in a manner that enables users of general purpose financial             IFRS Standards around
                   reports to understand the connections between the items to which the information relates and the                     the world
                   connections between disclosures provided by the entity.
                                                                                                                                        The IFRS Foundation
Core content       An entity is required to provide disclosures about:                                                                  and its boards
                   • Governance—the governance processes, controls and procedures the entity uses to monitor and                        IFRS Accounting Standards,
                     manage sustainability-related risks and opportunities                                                              Interpretations and
                                                                                                                                        Practice Statements
                   • Strategy—the approach the entity uses to manage sustainability-related risks and opportunities
                                                                                                                                      IFRS Sustainability Disclosure
                   • Risk management—the processes the entity uses to identify, assess, prioritise and monitor                        Standards
                     sustainability-related risks and opportunities
                                                                                                                                        IFRS Foundation projects
                   • Metrics and targets—the entity’s performance in relation to sustainability-related risks and
                     opportunities, including progress towards any targets the entity has set, or is required to meet by law or         Deloitte IFRS resources
                     regulation                                                                                                         Contacts
                   IFRS S1 sets out objectives for each of these aspects, and disclosure requirements to achieve those
                   objectives.
Sources of         IFRS S1 specifies which sources of guidance an entity should or may refer to and consider in addition to IFRS
guidance           Sustainability Disclosure Standards.

IFRS Sustainability Disclosure Standards                                                                                                   Introduction
                                                                                                                                           Deloitte Accounting
                                                                                                                                           Research Tool (DART)
Location of         An entity is required to provide disclosures required by IFRS Sustainability Disclosure Standards as part of its
disclosures         general purpose financial reports.                                                                                     Our IAS Plus website

Timing of           An entity is required to report its sustainability-related financial disclosures at the same time and for the same     IFRS Standards around
reporting           reporting period as its related financial statements.                                                                  the world
Comparative         Unless another IFRS Sustainability Disclosure Standard permits or requires otherwise, an entity is required            The IFRS Foundation
information         to disclose comparative information in respect of the preceding period for all amounts disclosed in the                and its boards
                    reporting period. If such information would be useful for an understanding of the sustainability-related
                    financial disclosures for the reporting period, the entity is also required to disclose comparative information        IFRS Accounting Standards,
                    for narrative and descriptive sustainability-related financial information.                                            Interpretations and
                                                                                                                                           Practice Statements
Judgements,         An entity is required to disclose information to enable users of general purpose financial reports to
uncertainties and   understand the judgements, apart from those involving estimations of amounts, that the entity has made               IFRS Sustainability Disclosure
errors              in the process of preparing its sustainability-related financial disclosures and that have the most significant      Standards
                    effect on the information included in those disclosures.
                                                                                                                                           IFRS Foundation projects
                    In addition, an entity is required to disclose information to enable users of general purpose financial reports
                                                                                                                                           Deloitte IFRS resources
                    to understand the most significant uncertainties affecting the amounts reported in its sustainability-related
                    financial disclosures.                                                                                                 Contacts

                    Furthermore, an entity is required to correct material prior period errors by restating the comparative
                    amounts for the prior period(s) disclosed unless it is impracticable to do so.

IFRS S2           Climate-related Disclosures                                                                                          Our IAS Plus website
Overview          Sets out the requirements with the objective of requiring an entity to disclose information about climate-           IFRS Standards around
                  related risks and opportunities that is useful to primary users of general purpose financial reports in making       the world
                  decisions relating to providing resources to the entity.
                                                                                                                                       The IFRS Foundation
Scope             IFRS S2 applies to climate-related risks to which the entity is exposed, which are climate-related physical and
                                                                                                                                       and its boards
                  transition risks, and climate-related opportunities available to the entity.
Governance        The objective of climate-related financial disclosures on governance is to enable users of general purpose           IFRS Accounting Standards,
                  financial reports to understand the governance processes, controls and procedures an entity uses to monitor,         Interpretations and
                  manage and oversee climate-related risks and opportunities.                                                          Practice Statements
Strategy          The objective of climate-related financial disclosures on strategy is to enable users of general purpose           IFRS Sustainability Disclosure
                  financial reports to understand an entity’s strategy for managing climate-related risks and opportunities.         Standards
Risk management   The objective of climate-related financial disclosures on risk management is to enable users of general
                                                                                                                                       IFRS Foundation projects
                  purpose financial reports to understand an entity’s processes to identify, assess, prioritise and monitor
                  climate-related risks and opportunities, including whether and how those processes are integrated into and           Deloitte IFRS resources
                  inform the entity’s overall risk management process.
                                                                                                                                       Contacts
Metrics and       The objective of climate-related financial disclosures on metrics and targets is to enable users of general
targets           purpose financial reports to understand an entity’s performance in relation to its climate-related risks and
                  opportunities, including progress towards any climate-related targets it has set, and any targets it is required
                  to meet by law or regulation.

Cross-industry    An entity is required to provide information in a manner that enables users of general purpose financial
metrics           reports to understand the connections between the items to which the information relates and the                         Our IAS Plus website
                  connections between disclosures provided by the entity.                                                                  IFRS Standards around
Core content      An entity, regardless of the industry in which it operates, is required to disclose specified metrics in respect of:     the world

                  •   Greenhouse gases                                                                                                     The IFRS Foundation
                  •   Climate-related transition and physical risks                                                                        and its boards
                  •   Climate-related opportunities
                                                                                                                                           IFRS Accounting Standards,
                  •   Capital deployment

                                      liabilities.                                                                        the world
                                      The new Standard is proposed to apply when the entity is party to a regulatory      The IFRS Foundation
                                      agreement that determines the regulated rate the entity can charge for the          and its boards
                                      goods or services it supplies to customers.
                                                                                                                          IFRS Accounting Standards,
                                      Regulatory assets and liabilities arise when part or all of the total allowed       Interpretations and
                                      compensation for goods or services supplied in one period is charged to             Practice Statements
                                      customers through the regulated rates for goods or services supplied in a
                                      different past or future period. An entity recognises all regulatory assets and     IFRS Sustainability

                                                                                                                          Disclosure Standards
                                                                                                                         IFRS Foundation projects
                                      Regulatory assets and liabilities would be measured at historical cost, modified
                                      for subsequent measurement by using updated estimates of the amount and             Deloitte IFRS resources
                                      timing of future cash flows. The estimated future cash flows of a regulatory
                                      asset or liability would be discounted to their present value by using the          Contacts
                                      regulatory interest rate.

Practice      Management Commentary     In May 2021, the IASB published ED/2021/6 Management Commentary, proposing
Statement 1                             a revision of the Practice Statement, as it has decided it should play a more           IFRS Accounting Standards,
                                        active role in wider corporate reporting. In particular, as specialists in financial    Interpretations and
                                        reporting, it concluded it is well placed to provide a link between financial and       Practice Statements
                                        non-financial information. The IASB determined that an appropriate way to do
                                        this is a wholesale revision of the Practice Statement.                                 IFRS Sustainability
                                                                                                                                Disclosure Standards
                                        One of the key points that the IASB wishes to address is promoting alignment
                                        between financial and ‘other’ information disclosed by an entity. This new             IFRS Foundation projects
                                        approach to management commentary could both encourage and support                      Deloitte IFRS resources
                                        further change, serving as an anchoring point for other updated standards and
                                        frameworks, for example integrated reports or management reports required               Contacts
                                        by jurisdictions.

                                        The IASB is expected to decide the direction of the project in Q4 of 2023.

Cross-cutting   Disclosure Initiative—        In July 2021, the IASB published ED/2021/7 Subsidiaries without Public
                Subsidiaries without Public   Accountability: Disclosures. In the ED, the IASB proposes a new IFRS Standard         Our IAS Plus website
                Accountability: Disclosures   that would permit a subsidiary to provide reduced disclosures when applying           IFRS Standards around
                                              IFRS Standards in its financial statements. A subsidiary would be eligible for the    the world
                                              reduced disclosures if it does not have public accountability and its ultimate or
                                              any immediate parent produces consolidated financial statements available for         The IFRS Foundation
                                              public use that comply with IFRS Standards.                                           and its boards

                                              The new Standard would be optional for subsidiaries that are eligible. It would       IFRS Accounting Standards,
                                              set out the disclosure requirements for subsidiaries that elect to apply it and       Interpretations and
                                              the disclosure requirements in IFRS Standards that do not apply and are               Practice Statements
                                              replaced by the draft Standard.                                                       IFRS Sustainability
                                              The final Standard is expected in 2024.

Accounting      Review                        for SMEs Accounting Standard. The ED proposes several amendments to the
Standard                                      Standard, including alignment of the Standard with the Conceptual Framework,
                                              IFRS 3, IFRS 10 and IFRS 15, partial alignment of the Standard with IFRS 9 and
                                              IFRS 11 and addition of a section on fair value measurement.
